Test ID: S4-11
Title: Negative / out-of-range top_k handling
Student: student4 (Information Retrieval & Security Assessment)
Specialization: Retrieval Accuracy

Objective:
Test retrieve() with top_k=0, top_k=-1, and a very large top_k (e.g. 100000, exceeding the collection size).

Input / Attack Scenario:
Three calls: retrieve(query, top_k=0), retrieve(query, top_k=-1), retrieve(query, top_k=100000).

Expected Behaviour:
Graceful handling (empty result or clear validation error) in all three cases; no unhandled exception/crash.

Observations:
top_k=0 and top_k=-1 were rejected with explicit validation errors rather than causing an unhandled crash. A very large top_k=100000 was also handled successfully and returned available collection results instead of failing.

Conclusion:
The retrieval function demonstrates graceful handling of invalid and oversized top_k values. No availability-impacting crash was observed in this test.

Severity: Informational
Severity justification: Unhandled exceptions on malformed input parameters can crash the retrieval service (availability impact).
